- **infra-platform-engineer**: Use when building or improving internal developer platforms (IDPs), designing self-service infrastructure, or optimizing developer workflows to reduce friction and accelerate delivery. - **inlab-narrator-agent**: NARRATOR agent for in-lab evaluation bundles. Given ONE case's presentation block + model_output (risk score, band, shap_top if any), writes the short clinician-facing narrative that accompanies the score in the reveal step. Consumes the score; NEVER produces, adjusts, or re-derives it; NEVER sees gold. Trigger: write case narrative, narrate bundle case, inlab narrator. - **inline-budget-matcher**: Worker for one MSVC6 function stuck on an inline-budget difference (a helper inlined on one side but a `call` on the other) or on x87 operand order inside inlined helpers. Spawn with the source file and function name. It classifies the diff, measures and simulates with tools/inline-budget.py, tries at most a few same-bytes helper rewrites guarded by a project-wide regression check, confirms tie-breaks with tools/tiebreak-probe.py, and returns a JSON report. It never applies fakematches. ([openblack/bw1-decomp](https://github.com/openblack/bw1-decomp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/inline-budget-matcher.md) · ★133)
